- Read-only inspection of the encyclopedia-related PostgreSQL function definitions and ACLs distinguished MIFASK-specific functions from project-wide Supabase advisor findings. `ie_record_analytics(event_kind text, article_slug text)` is SECURITY DEFINER with `search_path=public`, executable by anon and authenticated. ### QuranEnc publication-conditions review — 2026-11-26

- Reviewed the official QuranEnc API documentation: https://quranenc.com/en/home/api/translations and https://quranenc.com/en/home/api/session/properties.
- QuranEnc states that translation contents may be downloaded and republished subject to conditions: do not modify, add to, or remove content; clearly identify the publisher/source; include the translation version; retain required transcript information in the document; notify QuranEnc about translation notes; keep the translation updated to the latest source version; and do not display inappropriate advertisements alongside the translation.
- The translation-list API documents `version` and `last_update` fields, which can support future freshness checks. The live list endpoint could not be retrieved in this run, so the current English Saheeh release remains unverified; the database still records v1.1.2.
- These published conditions concern translation content. They do not establish permission for a separate Qur'an transliteration dataset. No transliteration import or translation update was performed. Before claiming compliance, verify the site's attribution/version/transcript display and compare all 6,236 stored translations and footnotes against the exact current source release.

### Safe code remediation committed in this change
- Hadith bulk import now stops with HTTP 409 `HADITH_IMPORT_RIGHTS_REVIEW_REQUIRED` unless the server-side `MIFASK_HADITH_IMPORT_RIGHTS_CONFIRMED=true` flag is explicitly set after rights, exact edition and provenance evidence have been documented and approved. The default is disabled; this run did not set or read any environment secret and did not invoke the import endpoint.
- Malik source URL is now pinned to repository commit `df57907be35291c91ad6a6691180e22ca9920784`; Riyad remains pinned to `70b83d6d21995bb32f8d7271cd75501be5a922a`. Future import manifests record the exact source revision, repository licence/readme references where applicable, `source_hash:null`, `source_hash_status`, `license_terms_url:null`, and `rights_review_status:"unresolved"`. The repository licence is explicitly not treated as underlying text permission.
- Quran import now requires QuranEnc to provide a non-empty `version`; it no longer substitutes a last-update timestamp or generic placeholder as the edition version. Future import manifests record the exact translation source URL, QuranEnc API terms URL, provider `last_update`, explicit unavailable source hash status, and separate AlQuranCloud Arabic/Surah metadata source and terms URL. Each verse records its per-Surah QuranEnc source URL. No existing import manifest or verse was rewritten.
- `scripts/route-smoke.mjs` now checks the Quran import version/provenance markers, Hadith rights gate, source commit pins, unresolved-rights metadata, and that the rights gate occurs before upstream fetch. These are static regression guards; the Git-triggered production build will execute them.
